Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The systems variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 565 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 220 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by systems generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 565, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 220. QED.
